<h2 class="h3">Welcome to Halo Lady Hair!</h2>
<div class="rte rte-setting featured-row__subtext"><p></p>
<p>We a professional human hair products supplier founded in 2012, Anemone hair is one of our brands for young girls . Our factory is located in Xuchang, Henan, China, sales headquarters in Guangzhou, there are 30-50 sales, operate 14 online stores on Taobao, DHgate, AliExpress, Alibaba, Ebay, Amazon. We specialize in the design, production and marketing of the highest quality, 100% human hair, hair extension, virgin hair extensions, wigs, remy virgin hair, Closure, bulk hair bundles and so on. 100% raw materials are imported from Brazil, Europe, Peru, India, Malaysia and other countries. We also provide customized customer logo, color, size, and OEM and do drop shipping.</p></div>
